# Full TSMOM Trade Record Book (Long-Only)

Universe: 20 Israeli stocks (TASE via Yahoo \".TA\")

Timeframe: **2008-01-01 → 2026-01-11** (monthly rebalance on first trading day of month)

Strategy:
- Signal: 12-1 momentum (binary)
- Long-only: negative signal => 0 (cash), no shorts
- Volatility scaling: target vol **40%** (EWMA vol, 60D center-of-mass)
- Rebalance: monthly

Portfolio settings:
- Initial capital: **50,000 NIS**
- Commission: **5 NIS** per execution (Buy/Sell)
- Shares: whole numbers only

Execution assumptions:
- Yahoo \".TA\" prices are typically quoted in **agorot**; this report converts all TASE prices to **NIS** by dividing by 100.
- Trades execute at **daily close** on the rebalance day (or the next available trading day for that symbol within the same month).
- Exposure scaling is computed per-asset; to remain cash-only (no leverage), positive scaled exposures are **normalized to sum to 100%** each rebalance.
